| `mods_ui` | Client, main thread. Focus-free Mods workspace: `status` / `open` / `close` / `back` / `select` / `search` / `scroll` / `widget_read` / `widget_click` / `native_tab` / `resolution` / `rects` / `window` / `scene` / `select_tab` / `hit` / `tabs` / `menu` / `keys` / `nav_back` / `nav_forward` / `nav_state` (KEYS4: [back and forward](mods-menu.md#back-and-forward) through the navigation keybinds, and the history; they need no open workspace, nor do `keys op=conflict` / `conflict-choose`). `settings_places` (settings organization stage 2; light read, no open workspace needed) returns `{settingId: {home, places[], value, display, known, store, source}}` for every placed setting of Progenitor's own ([mods-menu.md](mods-menu.md#where-progenitors-settings-are-drawn)); every settings row's readback carries `settingId`, `home` and `alsoAt`. GRAYCLICK1 (live 2026-10-14): `widget_click`, `dropdown_choose`, `radio_choose` and `dialog_choose` refuse a grayed control (its handle not interactable, a cell drawn disabled, a row that is not interactable) with ok:false and `'<caption>' is disabled (not interactable); not clicked: <the row's reason>`, and run nothing; before, the bridge dispatched the slot itself and reported `invoked:1` (fix source-checked and game-free tested, not live-verified). `ui_input` clicks already went through the pointer's own interactable check. DROPDOWN1: `widget_click label=<caption>` on a dropdown opens (or closes) its list and writes nothing; `dropdown_choose {label, value, pane?}` opens the dropdown captioned `label` as a click does and picks the option whose value or caption is `value` (one write when it differs; `{label, value, written, dropdown}`); RADIO1: `radio_choose {label, value, pane?}` chooses the line of the radio list captioned `label` whose value or label is `value`, as a click on that line (one write when it differs; `{label, value, written, radio}`), and `widget_read` gives a radio list `current`, `currentLabel` and `options [{value, label, description, selected, mark, part}]`; `widget_read` gives each dropdown `dropdown {open, options [{value, label, description, current}], highlight, hovered}` (a radio list also reports `hovered`, -1 for none). RADIOCLICK1: `click_at {row}` or `click_at {x, y}` (client pixels, as `hit`; no open workspace needed) clicks through the pointer route without OS input or focus: the event system's raycast picks the topmost object at the row's center or the point, the first click handler up its parents (button, toggle, event trigger) takes the click as a pointer click does, and it returns `{x, y, unityX, unityY, row, hit, ours, handler, handlerKind, invoked, why?}` (ok only when `invoked`). Unlike `widget_click` and `radio_choose`, which fire the slot directly, it fails where a player's click would (`handler: null`, the click swallowed by the object that was hit). `widget_click` on a row key now refuses a row no pointer can click (`reachable: false`), and a cell row's readback has `rowClick`. OPTIONHOVER1: `hover {label, option, pane?}` puts the pointer over the option (value or label) of the dropdown or radio list captioned `label`, opening a closed dropdown's list first as a click does (no write); `hover {label, leave: true}` moves it off the options. It returns `{label, kind: dropdown\|radio, hovered, open, option {index, value, label, description}, details}`. `widget_read` also returns a top-level `details`: the description column of the settings page on screen (the raw settings page, else the Preferences, Server or mod page) as `{row, source: option\|hover\|selected\|first\|none, heading, body, notes, tone, option, optionLabel, optionDescription, text}`, or null. DIALOGS3: `dialog_choose` takes `args.choice` = `yes` | `no` | `cancel` | a button's caption or cell key (ignoring case) | an index (a Choose's option; on other dialogs the button's screen position, so a one-button dialog's is `0`) and presses that button of the open dialog through its click slot (no open workspace needed; `ok: false` with no dialog or no such button, the error listing the dialog's choices; the readback's `choices` lists them too); `dialog_type {text}` types into the open Prompt's field as a player would (the field draws it; readback `typed`, `field`, `mainEnabled`); with a dialog open, `widget_click label=…` without `pane`/`index` clicks the dialog's button first ([ui.md](ui.md#the-games-popup-look-dialogs2)). `select_tab` takes `args.tab` = `mods` | `install` | `server` | `keybinds` | `preferences` (`configuration` is an alias; never a native tab). Three homes ([mods-menu.md](mods-menu.md#drawers-and-the-left-list)): `section {group, tab?}` is a left-list click (a group by caption or slug: scroll to it, open its drawer, highlight it); `drawer {group, open?}` opens, closes or toggles a settings drawer of the tab shown; both need an open workspace, and `nav_state` carries `sectionNav`; `keys {op, target?, chord?}` drives the [Keybinds tab](mods-menu.md#keybinds-tab), requires an open workspace, switches the workspace's own tab strip without closing or releasing capture, and reads `after` on the next Drain; `status.tab` reports the selected tab or `null` when closed. Refuses on the server (`mods_ui runs on the client side only`). Every action returns `{ok, action, before, after, detail, elapsedMs}`. `status` is exception-safe; its cost is bounded by owned bookkeeping (pane / observer / curtain handles) plus a `statusMs` field, not a scene-wide GameObject walk. Status fields include `modsCapture` (true only while the Mods workspace owns input capture/curtain — its WhileShown panes are shown), `inputCapture` (`Ui.IsCapturingInput`, which is true in-game even with Mods closed whenever any Progenitor panel holds the global context), and `capture` (alias of `inputCapture` for one release). `shortcut` is the configured Mods opener chord as `KeyChord` formats it (e.g. `F10`, `F10 + Ctrl`), or `null` if none is bound. `serverList` is `{state, count?}`: `available` (catalog received for the current epoch, with `count`), `unavailable` (cleared or never received), or `unknown`; `serverListEpoch` is that list's world-epoch stamp (or the last clear). The overview sentence `Server mod list unavailable…` stays on the workspace page, not on this status. Client world end, connection-ended, and handshake epoch change close through owned `ModsMenu.Close()` (log `Mods workspace: closed reason=world-ended` or `reason=server-context-cleared` once; curtain, capture and HUD observers released; main-menu entry re-evaluated). A Core-only refresh of a surviving world does not close. `close` is the owned `ModsMenu.Close()` (panes disposed, capture released; the curtain is dropped on the same frame on every close path — Close, Escape, lifecycle, Uninstall — and a tick self-heal drops a lingering backdrop while closed; stale owned roots from an earlier Core generation are destroyed at install/HUD attach). `back` is `ModsMenu.CloseOrBack()` (settings/custom page → mod overview, otherwise the same Close); `detail.path` reports which ran. `select` / `search` / `scroll` / `widget_*` refuse with `detail.error = "workspace closed"` (`ok: false`) when the workspace is not open, except a label-only `widget_click` (no `pane`, no `index`) while a dialog is open, which clicks that dialog's button or check line (a dialog over the HUD). `open`/`close`/`back`/`native_tab`/`resolution` schedule the `after` status on the **next Drain** (`MainThread.PostDelayed(0)` + `DeferredResult`; the game thread is never slept). Same-Drain `after` is read after the action; if it would report open-without-curtain, the op uses that deferred path and sets `detail.readback = "next-drain"`. `open.site` is `main-menu` / `top-tab` (owned button `onClick`), `shortcut` (`ModsMenu.Toggle`), or `standalone` (`ModsMenu.OpenStandalone`: the workspace window without the FullscreenMenu route, the shortcut's fallback window). In a world `shortcut` routes through the FullscreenMenu on the MODS tab ([Shortcut in a world](mods-menu.md#shortcut-in-a-world)): it returns `ok` with `detail.routedThroughMenu = true` while that open is still pending, and the workspace opens a few frames later. `open site=top-tab` from gameplay follows the player path: if the owned button is not attached, it brings up FullscreenMenu through `HUDMenuManager.GoToMenu` (the same call NativeModsEntry already uses), then polls up to 120 Drains (`NativeModsEntry.FullscreenMenuAttachFrames`) for `progenitor-ui-core-mods-tab` through that deferred path and invokes the owned `onClick` once. `detail.menuOpenedBy` is `HUDMenuManager.GoToMenu` or `already-up`. If the button never attaches, `ok` is false, `detail.error` is `top-tab button not attached`, and `detail.menu` is the observed HUD menu type; when this op requested FullscreenMenu it closes it through the same `HUDMenuManager.GoToMenu` call (`None`) and sets `detail.menuRestored`. `native_tab` fires `OuterTabEntry.OnPointerClick` and the observed `EventTrigger.OnPointerClick`. `rects` and `window` are same-Drain reads and do not require an open workspace. `rects` also reports `detail.shortcut` (the same configured opener chord as status). `rects` returns client-area pixel rectangles (origin top-left — the space observe.ps1 `click`/`move` use unless `-ScreenCoords`) plus the Unity screen-space rect (origin bottom-left) and the canvas scale used, for the owned main-menu and top-tab Mods buttons, each native outer tab (name, index), close/back, search, each visible sidebar row (plugin id, label), list and content scrollbar track and handle, each visible content widget (kind, label, input/toggle/apply sub-controls). Each rect has `visible`, `sortingOrder` (the control's canvas sorting order), and `interactable` (raycast target and not covered by an owned overlay that renders above it). Occlusion is computed only against owned surfaces this op can enumerate: an overlay occludes a control only when it overlaps the rect and sorts above it (canvas sorting order, then sibling order on a tie). Owned overlays sit in one canvas band, lowest first: pointer blocker 4997, workspace backdrop 4998, panes 4999, then 5000 (`ModsCanvasBand.Consent`, derived from `UiOwnership.OverlaySorting`; the consent prompt that used it was removed 2026-10-05), so the blocker never occludes owned workspace controls; it does occlude native tabs and the parked page it covers. `rects` detail also reports `framePath` (`native-container` / `native-container-capped` / `fallback`), `nativeFound` + `nativeWhy`, the `native-container` row (in game: `FullscreenMenu.ViewParent`, read by `TransformPoint` of two local corners — `GetWorldCorners` returns zeros through this interop), the backdrop's **clipped visible** rect with `drawable`, `blockerSorting` / `backdropSorting` / `panelsSorting` / `consentSorting` (still reported; no prompt uses that band since 2026-10-05), `nativeCanvasMax` (owned `overlay-N` canvases excluded by ancestry), `titleViewHidden` and `titleVideoPlaying` (title site: only the menu chrome is hidden, the background video keeps playing), `playBackdrop` `{present, source: prefab|live-clone|none, sprite, material, shader}` (the Play view's own `MenuBackgroundImage` cloned behind the workspace at the title), `hostedViewHidden` and `outerTabVisual` (in game: the previously selected native page is deactivated and its tab visual cleared while Mods is open; restored on every exit path). `scene` is a read-only, same-Drain, bounded probe (depth ≤ 6 under `MainMenuCanvasBase.MenuParent`, 2048-object cap with `truncated`) returning `view`, the ambience/music volume objects, `titleView` (incl. video players' `isPlaying`), `menuParent.children[]` with interesting components and each Image/RawImage's `sprite`/`material`/`shader`, `cameras[]`, scene `roots[]` and a `stamp`; it never mutates state. Live on 2026-09-11 (client generations 4–7). When occluded, `occludedBy` is `curtain` and `occluderOrder` is that overlay's sorting order. (The consent overlay's rows, the `consentPromptOpen` readback and `occludedBy=consent` were removed 2026-10-05 with the consent prompt.) `window` returns the OS client-area size and position (`GetClientRect`/`ClientToScreen` on the game window handle, the same pair observe.ps1 uses), `Screen.width`/`height`, `Screen.fullScreenMode`, and the primary display size, so a resolution change can be checked against the real client area rather than `Screen` alone. `resolution.mode` accepts `windowed` (`FullScreenMode.Windowed`) as well as `window` / `fullscreenwindow` / `exclusive`; its next-Drain `detail.window` is that OS readback. Source-checked, not live-verified. | this row `tabs` is a read-only same-Drain outer-tab probe; each native/owned row includes `graphics[]` (`path`, `type` Image\|TMP, `enabled`, `activeInHierarchy`, `rgba` four floats at 3 decimals, `sprite`, TMP `fontAsset`/`material`); the owned `highlightImage` is the child named `highlight`; `tabs` detail also carries `selectedOuterTab` (the `CurrentView.State` / `_SelectedOuterTab` page name), and a native row's `isSelected` is that page when Mods is closed, not the entry flag; `detail.hold` `{held, cleared, tickRuns, lateRuns, lateSkippedWhy, painters, buttons, lastCaptureFrame, adoptedRows, hostedRestore}` reports the hold's per-frame counters (reset on release; `hostedRestore` = `restored` | `skipped-different-page` | `none`, decided by native pointer identity). `status.curtain` is `{present, raycastTarget}` and `rects` includes a `curtain` row `{present, active, raycastTarget, alpha, sortingOrder, x, y, width, height}`. `status.route` / `rects.detail.route` is `native` while an owned eighth outer tab is registered with the game's `FullscreenMenuMapper` (NATIVE1, guarded prototype) and `imitation` otherwise; `nativeRoute` is `{state, viewsIndex, listIndex, reason, callback, selected, mounted, adapter, attempts, pruned, staleReason}` (NATIVE4: `pruned` counts stale owned state-7 entries this attach removed, `staleReason` how they were recognized — `page-name` | `page-dead` | `tab-name` | `tab-dead`; Core unload now always detaches the registration, log `detached (native route, core-unload)` — a HUD rebuild had destroyed the owned page so the dead-menu detach branch skipped `RestoreViews` and every later generation refused with `competing-slot-7:views@7`); NATIVE5: `nativeRoute` also carries `retryable` and `nextAttemptOn` (`menu-open` | `none`) — every owned leftover is pruned by name before the shape check (an `_OuterTabs` entry or `OuterTabGroup` child named `progenitor-ui-core-mods-tab`, a `Views` record whose page is `progenitor-ui-core-mods-page`, whatever its state), Drop/unload also removes the owned entry from the mapper's `_OuterTabs`, `pruned`/`staleReason` survive a refused registration, and a shape/count refusal is retried on later menu opens up to 5 attempts (only `adapter-unavailable` and a live foreign state-7 owner are terminal) (`attempts` = attach attempts on the current host; while the mapper's `_Menu` is null/dead the attach is retried every 30 frames up to 20 times; a mismatch reason reads `validate:mapper-menu-mismatch:mapper=…,found=…,mapperPath=…,foundPath=…,mapperRunning=…`, and when the mapper holds a different live menu the registration targets that menu and the readback carries `menu-authority:mapper,…`) — `state` 7, the indices are the live positions of the owned record/entry (−1 on the imitation route), `reason` names the failed invariant or step (`adapter-unavailable`, `mapper-missing`, `validate:…`, `clone-donor:…`, `create-page:…`, `append-views:…`, `wire-click:…`, `publish:…`, `expose:…`, `reconcile:…`, `detach-held:…`), `callback` is `ours` | `native` | `held` | `none`, `adapter` is Bootstrap `UiActionSlot.Capability` (0 on an older Bootstrap → imitation route). On the native route `open site=top-tab` selects the owned page through the typed mapper `SetState(7)` (`detail.route = native`, `detail.steps = SelectOwned+OpenWorkspace`) instead of a uGUI onClick, `close` returns the selection to the saved native page (`steps = CloseWorkspace+ReturnSelection`, `detail.nativeReturn` on a failed return), `native_tab` with no FullscreenMenu up is a no-op (`detail.reason = no-menu`), the workspace chrome stays a top-level overlay (`nativeRoute.mounted` is false), a closing menu detaches the registration after returning the selection (log `menu-closed`; `detach-held:…` only while the FullscreenMenu is still the HUD's current menu), and a doubled native outer list detaches with `outer-list-doubled:N`. `status.fontDonor` is `{font, path, source}` (`source` = `asset-list` / `options-label` / `scene-sweep`) for the cached TMP font (null until the first `FindFont`); `status.fontAssets` (only when `args.fonts=true`) lists each loaded `TMP_FontAsset` with `name`, `pointSize`, `scale`, `atlasPadding`, `advanceN`, `advanceI` (or null + `why`); `widget_read` / `rects` rows that have a TMP include `tmp` (`font`, `material`, `fontSize`, `fontScale`, `characterSpacing`, `wordSpacing`, `alignment`, `horizontalAlignment`, `fontStyle`, `enableAutoSizing`, `wordWrapping`, `overflow`, `lossyScale`, `rectW`, `rectH`, `richText`, `preferredWidth`), read on demand. Each `tabs` native/owned row also carries `interactable` / `isHovered` / `raycastTarget`, and each `graphics[]` row `crossFadeAlpha`, `canvasRendererColor` (rgba from `canvasRenderer.GetColor()`), `visibleAlpha` (`color.a × canvasRenderer.GetAlpha()`), `rect` `{x,y,w,h}` (screen px via `TransformPoint` of two corners) and `siblingIndex`, plus local RectTransform `anchoredPosition` / `sizeDelta` / `anchorMin` / `anchorMax` / `pivot` `{x,y}`. In game `rects` also reports a `native-background` row (the hosted page's `Background`/`BackgroundImage` painter rect, read by `TransformPoint`; `found`, `why`) and `detail.nativeBackgroundWhy` (`live` / `cached` / a miss reason); `detail.firstShowFrame` and `detail.layoutBeforeShow` record when the workspace was first presented and how many layouts ran before it (in game the workspace stays hidden until the native frame is read, so `layoutBeforeShow` is 0 on a normal open; the title site presents its fallback immediately); the workspace frame, backdrop and panes size to that rect (falling back to `native-container` when it is missing). `menu` with `args.op` = `status` / `open` / `close` (GAMEMENU1) drives the game's full-screen menu; see [bridge-ops-ui.md](bridge-ops-ui.md#the-games-full-screen-menu-menu-op). `menu` without `args.op` is a read-only same-Drain probe (depth ≤ 6 under the `FullscreenMenu` root, 1024-object cap with `truncated`) listing every Image/RawImage/TMP with `path`, `type`, `enabled`, `activeInHierarchy`, `rect`, `sprite`, `rgba`, `sortingOrder`, `zone` (`viewparent` / `hosted` / `outer-tab` / `elsewhere`), plus the same shape for the hosted instance. In game the workspace frame is the union of `ViewParent` and the menu-root footer chrome below it (≤ 120 units, not wider than 1.2× ViewParent, outer-tab group excluded) rather than the hosted `BackgroundImage`; `rects` `detail.frameExtentWhy` names the native objects that set the frame bottom. `widget_read` on the Install listing page: the first description row includes `readme` `{state, chars, reason, blocks}` (`blocks` = README row count; the README renders one row per markdown block) and the changelog row `changelog` `{state, chars, reason}`; the listing's pinned header row (`kind: header`) carries `author`, `version`, `released`, `action {label, decision, reason}` and `installState {state: not-installed|pending|loaded|managed-elsewhere|update-available, by, files[], loadedVersion}`; the header row's `action` also carries `phase` (`idle|downloading|inspected|reviewing|executing|done|failed|cancelled`), `progress`, `error`, `target`, `plan {decision, target, reason, hash, approved, pluginGuid, loadsIn, companions[], dependencies[{package, satisfiedBy}], ledger {client, server}}` and `result {path, sha256, alreadyPresent}` — `widget_click` on the primary (`Download and check` → planner label → `Confirm`) drives the flow (download/inspect never executes the package; Confirm writes to `BepInEx/progenitor-pending/`); the Install sidebar count row is a button-styled row (`kind: button`); on Configuration, the local-server rows (toggle `Install Progenitor on this server`, its status note, `Update`, and the removal prompt's heading/body/`Remove`/`Cancel`) carry `serverInstall {state: not-installed|installed|update-available|refused, version, reason, ledgerFiles, promptOpen, line}` (and, MODS2, the sibling `progenitorUninstall {requested, requestedUtc, files, promptOpen, line}` for **Uninstall Progenitor from this client** — since settings organization stage 4 on Progenitor's page's **Remove Progenitor from this PC** / **Removal requested** rows instead of the Configuration tab — a `BepInEx/progenitor-uninstall.json` marker Bootstrap honors at the next start before Core loads: ledgered mods, Progenitor's own files, caches and the ledger deleted, its own DLL scheduled with MoveFileEx, the game then starts without Progenitor; BepInEx/core and other tools' files never touched) — the install copies the client's own Bootstrap/Core image into the server folder and records every file it writes in `BepInEx/progenitor-install.json`; removal deletes exactly the ledgered files (mods Progenitor installed included) after the prompt; both refuse while `VRisingServer.exe` runs from that folder and never start or stop a process (Progenitor-written pending/reload files are removable from the header; files under `BepInEx/plugins/` never are); the header row also carries `releasedSource` (`version`|`package`|`none`); `rects` measures the live header in client pixels: one `header` band, one `viewport`, `header-title` (`kind: label`), `action` (`kind: button`, label = caption, index 1 = Remove) and `header-reason`, header above and not overlapping the viewport. Install sidebar rows carry `side`, `sideSource` (`manifest`|`categories`|`description`|`unknown`), `searchScope` (`oneLiner`|`withReadme`), `currentUpdate`; the side row carries `filter: side`, `value` (`any`|`server`|`client`|`both`) and `caption`; the two toggle rows `filter` (`progenitor`|`currentUpdate`), `on`, `caption` (long wording), `currentUpdateTag` and `currentUpdateSource` (`config`|`adapter`|`unknown` — the current game update comes from the `[Install] CurrentUpdateTag` config key or the V Rising build→tag adapter, never from package edit dates; unknown hides nothing); the count row `matches`/`total`; the fetch button (`Fetch descriptions` / `Cancel (n/m)`) `prefetch {state, done, total, progress}`; the header row adds `updateWarning`, `currentUpdateTag` and `currentUpdateSource` (a package not tagged for the current game update is warned about, never blocked) (`loading` \| `ready` \| `unavailable`; the README and changelog are fetched on demand from Thunderstore's versioned experimental routes and rendered as plain text); Since LIST2 the Install tab is a list view and the sidebar/header rows above are superseded: `widget_read` rows are the tab strip, the `Search mods` input row, the `toolbar` cellrow (`count {shown, filtered, total}`, `descriptions {state, done, total}` for the automatic README fetch, and its `cells[]` incl. the Sort control), the `filters` cellrow (`side-any|server|client|both` chips, `No restart needed`, `Current update (<tag>)`, `Clear filters` only off the defaults), the `header` cellheader, one `cellrow` per visible listing (label = package id) carrying `listing {id, state: none|installing|pending|installed|update|tool|loader|blocked|removed, restart, side, loadsIn, label, reason, currentUpdate, searchScope}` and `cells[]` (`icon`, `name`, `author`, `side`, `version`, `released`, `downloads`, `update`, `install`, each with `kind`, `text`, `tone`, `visible`, `rect`), the open drawer's rows (`drawer: <id>` on the drawer cellrow; then the restart note, meta line, one-liner, README blocks, `More info`, `Configure in Mods`), and a `pending {count}` bar when any listing is pending; `widget_click` on a listing's label toggles its drawer, on its `install` cell runs DL1's flow (target chooser only with two targets). Live 2026-09-12 (client gen 8). LIST3: a `CellRow` may carry an `input` cell (`CellKind.Input`: `text` is the value, `placeholder` the empty hint, edits raise `OnChanged(cellKey, handler)` per keystroke and read back as `{kind: input, text, placeholder}`) — the Install search is the toolbar's first cell (`toolbar.search`); the Install pane is configured `MenuPaneMode.FullWidth` (whole frame width, no sidebar column, no pane title, no footer rows; `mods_ui` geometry reports `layout: full-width` and `contentWidth`); the index is served from `BepInEx/progenitor-cache/index.json` on open and refreshed once per open when older than 30 min. SERVERINST3: `serverInstall` also carries `process {running, pid, path, graceful, launchRecord, commandLine, connectedTo}` (`connectedTo` = `"<protocol>://<address>:<port>"` from Bootstrap's record of the `GameHelper.LaunchGame` join address, read through `BootstrapLink.LastConnectPort`; the gate compares that port with the folder's own port read from the running process's `-persistentDataPath` host settings and a mismatch names both values; a Bootstrap without the record answers `This Bootstrap does not record the joined server's port`; SERVERINST5) — `Start server` replays the launch record through `Process.Start` with the inherited `DOORSTOP_*` variables stripped from the child's environment (otherwise Doorstop skips injection in a child of the client and the server boots without BepInEx); live 2026-09-12 on the isolated test server: Stop (save, quit, record) then Start (BepInEx and the bridge up, client reconnected) PASS (command line PEB-read, image token dropped, ≤ 512 chars) and `stop {state: idle|requested|replied|saving|stopped|failed|save-timeout|not-admin|force-prompt, reason, states[]}` on the `Stop server`, `Stop server (force)`, `Start server` and force-prompt rows (SERVERINST4: the graceful stop travels on reserved Core event id `0x50727351` — request/reply with a nonce; the server refuses non-admins before any save, saves through Core's `WorldSaveTrigger` and quits only once the save file's write stamp advances, else `save-timeout`; source-checked and game-free tested). Live 2026-09-12 (client gen 9: list view, SERVERINST2 removal prompt). KEY2: `status.focus {field: mods-search|install-search|none, hotkeysBlocked, blockedActions[]}` — while an owned text field has focus the already-registered input context's per-frame mask also blocks the FullscreenMenu's per-menu open actions (the `ButtonInputAction` 18–26 block incl. `ClanMenu = 23` = P, and `Menu_TabLeft/Right/Minor` 1006–1009, client dump) with both `BlockKey` and `BlockKeyDown`; released by the shown-input pump every frame the field is not focused, on pump unsubscribe, workspace close, Core unload and blocker Dispose; `widget_read` input cells and the sidebar search rect carry `focused`. KEY3: that 599 mask alone was late — `FullscreenMenuMapper` registers its own context at 502 and reads the tab letters from `InputsUp` — so a second focus-scoped context at order 498 (`UiInputCapture.HotkeyGuardOrder`, registered and removed with the 599 context, sharing the `GetConsumedInputs` detour by instance pointer) masks the hotkey set ahead of it; `status.focus.guard {registered, order, masked}` (`masked` = applications in the last second). Live 2026-09-12 (client gen 4): with either search focused a real `P` only edits the text and the workspace stays open; with no field focused `P` opens Social as vanilla. ICON2: each Install listing row reads back `icon {state: ready|queued|loading|failed|missing|none, cached, error}` and the `toolbar` row `icons {ready, queued, loading, failed, inFlightUrl, lastError, fetches, stalledSeconds}` — disk-cached icons paint on their own lane ahead of the one-at-a-time network lane, a fetch times out after 15 s (`IconCache.FetchTimeout`) without parking the worker, failed URLs are re-asked after 30 s, and `ReleaseAll` restarts the worker (ICON2 live 2026-09-12 client gen 3: all rows ready after a sort, no stall). INSTORD1: ordinary Thunderstore BepInEx packages install on the restart route — approved layout (DLLs at the zip root or in one folder under `BepInEx/plugins/**`, exactly one `[BepInPlugin]` entry point, companions declaring none; `patchers/`/`core/`/`config/` and loose data files refused by name), identity read from metadata without executing (`[BepInPlugin]`, `[BepInProcess]` → `loadsIn`, `[BepInDependency]`), dependencies: BepInExPack satisfied by the running BepInEx, any other must be Progenitor-ledgered or a loaded plugin whose GUID a prior inspection of that package cached (`progenitor-cache/identity/…`) else `requires <namespace-name> — install it first`; files go to `progenitor-pending/index/` (companions flat beside the plugin) and Bootstrap promotes them into `progenitor-reload/` (never `plugins/`; an operator `progenitor-allow.txt`, when present, must list the DLL), every written file is ledgered in `<root>/BepInEx/progenitor-install.json` on the client and the server root (source `package:<Owner-Name>@<version>`), and **Remove** / **Remove · restart to unload** on a ledgered install deletes exactly those pending and promoted files and rows (`ServerInstall.RemovePackage`). Wiring: `ModsMenu` builds the catalog service with the validated server root and the client's loaded plugin ids, rebuilds it when the validated folder changes, and sets the pane's `LedgerProbe`. INSTORD2: after Bootstrap's promote the ledger is reconciled on every catalog open, install and remove (`ServerInstall.ReconcilePromoted`: a `pending` row whose file moved becomes the `progenitor-reload/` path as `mod`; a vanished one is dropped as `promote-lost`), `InstalledState.Scan` reads each root's `progenitor-install.json` so a ledgered package is `loaded` / `pending` (promoted, loads at the next start; or on the local server) / `update-available` without an inspection, listing rows carry `ledger {client, server, version}` and `files [{path, kind: pending|reload, root: client|server}]`, and a drawer's `action.identity {pluginGuid, pluginVersion, loadsIn, version}` comes from the identity cache at rest; Remove matches the package at any version. Live 2026-09-12 (client gen 7 / server gen 8): a mod → client and a third-party mod → local server installed from the tab, promoted at the restarts and loaded (mod log lines), Mods tab lists both; after deploy #57 (Remove captions dispatch to `OnRemoveClick`) Remove deleted each reload copy and ledger row and the next restarts loaded neither — install → promote → load → remove → unload verified on client and server. MODS1: `status.mods {shown, required, server}` reports the Mods tab's decided sidebar — the player's list (`progenitor` + `other` rows loaded on this client), then the server-only `On this server (N)` group, then a collapsed read-only `Required (N)` group at the bottom (MODS2: the BepInEx loader with its version, Progenitor and Progenitor's own plugins; no developer toggle) — and each `entries[]` row carries `provenance` (`progenitor|other|required|server`), `group` (`mods|server|required`) and `subtitle` — live 2026-09-13 (client gen 1 after the MODS2 Bootstrap restart): sidebar = the player's 6 mods then collapsed `Required (7)` (BepInEx, Progenitor, Dev Bridge, Sample + 3 ids), no checkbox, `status.mods {shown 6, required 7, server 0}`, the loader page reads `The plugin loader. Required by every mod; not managed here.`, the Install tab's BepInExPack row reads `Installed · loader`, and Configuration shows `Uninstall Progenitor from this client` (marker path not exercised); a `progenitor` row's page offers Remove / Remove · restart to unload through the Install tab's ledger-scoped remover and, when the index is newer, `Update available` with a jump to that listing; `other` rows read `Installed by another tool — remove it with that tool.` — live 2026-09-12 (client gen 5): default `5 mods found on this client. 6 developer mods hidden.` / `status.mods {shown 5, hiddenDeveloper 6}`, the `Developer mods` toggle flips both to 11/0 and back, the overview ends with `Install and update mods on the Install mods tab; remove Progenitor-installed mods from their page here.`; sidebar rows stay single-line captions (kit gap), the subtitle lives on the page and in `entries[].subtitle`. LIST4: the listing row's Install-column button is the single primary action (`Choose where to install`, disabled, while the `target:<key>` chooser is open; the drawer head is one `note` cell with the install fact and carries `scrolledTo {rowTop, viewportTop, viewportHeight}` after the reveal; `listing.action {…}` is the action object's home, `drawer:<key>.action` an alias for one release; `widget_click drawer:<key>/action` no longer resolves, `<key>/install` is the button); `mods_ui hit` now walks every cell row's clickable cells, so `clickWouldInvoke` names `<rowKey>/<cellKey>` (e.g. `target:<key>/client`) — before LIST4 it read the tab strip only, which is why the chooser chips probed as `no-hit` while real clicks worked; drawer glyphs are WGL4 `►`/`▼`; README blocks equal to the one-liner or the package name are dropped. HOT1: an `ApplyNow` plan with an `ApplyNowEligible` admission on a root this process runs is written by `Install/HotLoadExecutor.cs` straight into `progenitor-reload/` (ledger `mod`, `action.result.hotLoaded`), the reloader loads it within a second and Remove hot-unloads it (`hotUnloaded`); a `[Install] LocalIndexPath` folder's `index.json` (Thunderstore v1 shape, `file:///` URLs) merges in front of the fetched index through a file transport, and the Dev op `install_local` `{action: status|reload|qualify}` reports it and authors reviewed qualification records — HOT2 landed the Bootstrap admission snapshot (`ProcessHost.AdmissionSnapshotCapability = 1`; plain values: process side, Core generation, loader busy, prior generation pinned, loaded plugin identity, UnsafeHarmony mark, allowlist decision), the Core source through `BootstrapLink`, a pre-write recheck against the approved plan's loader state (`SnapshotChanged` refuses), the evaluator wired into the menu with derived compatibility identities, the `qualify` record writer and the `Side.Both` HotDemo sample; `listing.action.result` reads `{path, sha256, alreadyPresent, hotLoaded, serverPendingPath, rechecked}` and a Remove adds `removed {ok, noOp, hotUnloaded, files, skipped}`. Live 2026-09-12 (client gen 1 after the Bootstrap restart): the HotDemo dummy from a `file://` local index, qualified through `install_local qualify`, planned `ApplyNow` (`rechecked true`), was written to `progenitor-reload/` and loaded by the reloader within seconds (`Reload: loaded Progenitor Hot Demo 0.1.0`), and Remove hot-unloaded it (`Reload: unloaded … clean`) — hot-swap install PASS; HOT3: a ledgered `progenitor-reload/` copy is `listing.state: installed` with the button `Remove` and `restart: false` as soon as `PluginReloader.LoadedPluginNames()` reports its plugin (not only when the Mods-tab inventory names it), rows carry `listing.hotLoaded` and `installState.hotLoaded`, a `removed.hotUnloaded` Remove returns the row to `none` / `Install` immediately while the restart route keeps `removed`, and an install or remove that changed what this process loaded retakes `ModMenuInventory.Snapshot()` (`ModsMenu.NoteInventoryChanged`; a tab switch always retakes), so the Mods tab lists or drops the plugin in the same workspace open; a `removed.hotUnloaded` listing stays `none` for the session even while the reloader's report and the inventory snapshot lag, and one re-check is posted 2 s later (`InstallModsPane.HotUnloadRecheckSeconds`), dropped if the pane was released. LIST4's `scrolledTo` reports the offset actually written (read back off the ScrollRect); a row `rect` in the same readback may predate the layout pass — read rects on the next readback or trust the capture. HOT4: a `Both` install is admitted once per half (client with `Client`, server with `LocalServer`; a `Both`-scoped record covers either), the plan carries `plan.halves [{target, decision, executorAvailable, reason}]` with mixed halves valid (client hot, server pending), the listing readback gains `halves {client, server}` = `loaded|pending|none` and `serverHalfNote`, and ledger rows written under the plugin id (the progenitor.json route) read back and Remove takes both source spellings; the drawer's expand marker is `»` (U+25BA was also missing in the game font). DEPS1: `plan.dependencies[{package, version, satisfiedBy: installed|loaded|closure, decision, reason}]` and `plan.closureCount` (the packages an install writes before the root, dependencies first; a member already on the target has a null `decision`), `result.members[{package, version, path, hotLoaded, serverPendingPath}]`, and `removed.dependents[]` (ledgered packages whose own index listing names the removed one; removal refuses unless forced). MODSPAGE1: the Mods tab's mod page reads back one key per row — `page-title`, `page-meta {version, released, author}`, `page-oneliner`, `page-status {state: installed|pendload|penduninst, word}`, `page-action {label}`, `page-badges[]`, `page-warning` (only while the required-server warning shows), `page-store-link {url}`, `page-settings {kind: progenitor|none}`, `page-readme {state, chars, blocks}`, and the y/n rows `modPrompt {kind: uninstall|reactivate|cancel, open, row: heading|body|yes|no}`; every action asks that prompt and only its yes changes anything. `entries[]` rows carry `state` (the same three names) and `warning: bool`; a ledgered install nothing has loaded is listed as a `pendload` row. KIT1: the detail-page blocks — `panel.HeadBlock` (two-column head: title / meta with an inline toggle-button / wrapping one-liner / optional warning on the left; status word, action button, badges right-aligned on the same rows), `panel.Label(text, LabelTone)` (`Ok|Pending|Orange|Warn|Muted|Normal|Danger`, colors from `MenuPalette.LabelColor`), `panel.IconLink` (`»` glyph, readback `{kind: link, glyph, tooltip, url}`), `panel.Button(caption, marker, dot)` (readback `marker`, `dot`; `entries[].marker/.dot`) and `panel.Prompt` — are internal `UiKitBlocks` extension methods, not published API; the Mods page's head rows share readback keys per row (`page-title`+`page-status`, `page-meta`+`page-action`, `page-oneliner`+`page-badges`) each with `cells[].rect`; `mods_ui rects` does not measure head-block rows (use `widget_read` rects). Source-checked and game-free tested; live check follows. `tmp` remains the TMP snapshot. INSTALL2 (live 2026-09-13, deploy #70): Install listing rows read `listing.state` (`none`, `pending`, `installed`, `penduninst`, `blocked`, `tool`) with `restart`, `newTag` (true only for a file this process hot-loaded; the ledger's `hot` flag is cleared at the next process start, so the tag never survives a restart), `uninstallPending` and `rating`; `action.prompt {kind: install|uninstall|reactivate|cancelInstall, heading, body, chooser, yes, dependents}` is the in-pane y/n confirmation, clicked as `prompt:<key>/promptYes` or `/promptNo`, and `action.plan.closureCount` / `plan.dependencies[{package, version, satisfiedBy}]` / `result.members[{package, path, hotLoaded}]` (DEPS1) describe the dependency closure installed with the root; `removed.pending` is true when an ordinary loaded mod was marked with `<file>.uninstall` beside its reload copy (Bootstrap deletes both at the next start); the pending bar row reads `pending {count, uninstall, open, items[{key, state}]}` and its text expands on click to `pendingItem:<key>` rows. KIT1 (live 2026-09-13): the mod page hides the pane title (`ModPagePane.PaneTitleHidden`), its body rows read `align: left`, the head block's meta cells carry their leading `·` separator in the cell text (`· 2026-09-06`) while `page-meta.line` keeps the joined line, and the Like cell is sized to its caption. LINK1 (live 2026-09-13): the mod page's title row also carries `page-listing {matched: source|identity|id|name|none, key, rating}` — which rule resolved the listing (the install-ledger source, the inspected-identity cache, a listing id, or the plugin's own name spelling the package name), the listing key, and the provider's `rating_score`; a `name` match never drives Remove or a dependency decision. **Meaning changed with ABOUTSRC1:** `matched`, `key` and `listingVersion` describe the *candidate* listing the resolution found, whether or not the page shows it (before, `key` was the listing on show); `verified` (`ledger` | `manifest` | `unverified` | `none`), `versionMatch` and `shown` describe what the page *displays*: `shown` is true only for a `ledger` or `manifest` package match, and the listing's README is the About text only when `versionMatch` is also true. `rating` is no longer reported. `page-readme` gained `source` (`listing` | `embedded` | `beside` | `declared` | `description` | `none`); see [modules.md](modules.md#about-text-on-the-mod-page), and Install listing rows gained `listing.rating` from the same field. POLISH1 (live 2026-09-13, deploy #71): `pending` gains `sides` (`client` | `server` | `both` | `none`), read off where the ledgered files are, and the bar's sentence follows it (`restart the game` / `restart the local server` / `restart the game and the local server to apply`); `listing.state` is `installed` and `listing.halves.server` `loaded` once the local server has restarted and promoted its copy (a reconciled `mod` ledger row naming a `progenitor-reload/` file on the server root) — the Install tab reconciles both roots' ledgers at the top of every build, one `progenitor-install.json` stamp per root; `listing.restart` is `false` and `listing.hotLoaded` `true` for a ledgered `mod` copy the reloader reports loaded whose exact bytes the local qualification policy covers for this side, while `listing.newTag` stays `false` (the tag is session-scoped). Every string in a `mods_ui` reply is escaped to `\uXXXX` ASCII (`JavaScriptEncoder.Default`); a reply showing mojibake is being decoded wrongly after the bridge. MODSPOLISH1 (live 2026-09-13): provenance asks the install ledger before the framework-id prefix — a plugin a root's ledger lists as a `mod` (or the `pending` download that becomes one) reads `progenitor` and offers Uninstall even under `progenitor.` (the HotDemo dummy), so `required` is BepInEx plus the framework's own unledgered images (`Required (7)` live); `page-badges` carries only what is known (no `Unknown until checked` placeholder) and `page-oneliner` / `page-badges` are absent when neither is drawn; every head-block row and cell carries a `rect` in `widget_read`. CONSENT1 (live 2026-09-13; removed 2026-10-05 with the consent panel): the server-offer consent panel was an opaque, content-sized, centered dialog on its own raycast-blocking backdrop, laid out before it is shown (`Ui: consent panel opened host=… rows=N placedBeforeShow=true hidden=true placed=true frame=<n>`; `ModManifestConsentPanel.DescribeShow()`). POLISH2 (live 2026-09-13, deploy #72): `sort` is `released` by default — the Install tab opens on Recently released, newest `date_created` first with undated listings last; the toolbar cycles `released → downloads → name`; `entries[].state` is decided from the install-ledger rows a row carries (a `mod`-category `progenitor-reload/` copy on the local-server root reads `installed`, a pending copy `pendload`, an `uninstallPending` row `penduninst`) and the page's `Status:` word is the same decision; a server mod-list claim naming a plugin the ledger already names is folded into the ledger row, so `status.mods.server` counts only unmatched claims; `action.prompt.body` for an uninstall names the processes that hold the file on the same side rule as `pending.sides`. CONSENT2 (live 2026-09-13, deploy #73; removed 2026-10-05 with the consent panel, `Ui.SelfPlacePanel` remains): the consent prompt revealed only after the runtime's layout pass and is self-placed (`Ui.SelfPlacePanel`; log `re-flow skipped … at=(self-placed)`), the open line reads `… placedBeforeShow=<bool> hidden=true placed=true layoutFrame=<n|-1> revealFrame=<n|-1>` with a `consent panel revealed late …` line when the pass came later; no scrollbar or gutter when the rows fit; the operator-key line is gone; a decline is written to `BepInEx/config/progenitor-server-mods-declined.txt` (host key, id, version) and honored across Core reloads and restarts (`consent row skipped … declined earlier`), `ModManifestConsent.ForgetDecline(modId, hostKey?)` re-enables. INSTALLUX2 (live 2026-09-13, deploy #74): every Install-tab confirmation (and the Mods page's y/n prompts) opens as a centered modal dialog (`Ui.Dialog`; `action.prompt.presentation: "dialog"`, `promptClose` for a refusal, Escape = No) and `status.dialog {open, key, kind, footer}` is present in every status shape; `status.footer` is the Mods workspace's version line (`Progenitor <core> · Bootstrap <bootstrap>`, plus `· Dev <dev>` only while the dev bridge is loaded; empty while closed) and `status.dialog.footer` the open dialog's `Progenitor <core>` line (both source-checked and game-free tested, not live-verified); Installed rows draw a status word plus one button (`listing.actions[]` = `install` | `uninstall` | `reactivate` | `cancel`), `listing.newTag` is always false; the body reads `Client and server mod by …` for a Both listing; the Mods sidebar rebuilds on tab select after a ledger change or hot load. POLISH3 (live 2026-09-13, deploy #76): `action.removed.halves {client, server}` = `none` | `unloaded` | `removed` | `cancelled` | `marked` | `failed` — what one Uninstall's Yes did to each half (a server half on disk that could not be taken reports `failed`); the `»` store link ends at the head block's inset edge. POLISH4 (live 2026-09-13, deploy #77): ledger rows record `source: package:<listing key>@<version>` plus an optional `pluginId`, and every reader matches through `LedgerSpelling` (key, manifest id/package, owner-name, `pluginId`, identity cache), so a row written under the plugin id by an earlier build still reads back (`halves.server: pending`) and is uninstalled with the rest; a ledgered install nothing has loaded is captioned with the listing's name while `entries[].id` keeps the GUID. SETTINGS1: a mod page's `widget_read` adds `page-framework`, `page-header`, `page-section`, `page-setting`, `page-setting-note`, `page-setting-reset`, `page-settings-search` and `page-settings-reset-all` ([mod-settings.md](mod-settings.md#readback-mods_ui-widget_read)). TEXTTEST1 (source-checked and game-free tested, not live-verified): `field_focus {label, pane?}`, `field_blur`, `field_submit`, `type {text \| keys, via: os-text \| keys, perFrame?}`, `paste {text, repeat? \| length, seed?, measureFrames?}` and `perf {frames?}` drive and measure the workspace's text fields through the player's input paths with no OS input, focus or clipboard access ([bridge-ops-ui.md](bridge-ops-ui.md#mods_ui-text-field-tests-texttest1)). |

To open an install window in a test, serve the needed version from a local Thunderstore index: [test-rig.md](test-rig.md#install-window-from-thunderstore-tofu-and-legacy-cleanup). | — |
| `join_admin` | Both sides, main thread. JOINADMIN1 (join flow stage 3): client — open, read and drive the admins' Players joining popup (`read`, `open`, `close`, `refresh`, `escape`, `page`, `search`, `pick`, `recommend`, `unrecommend`, `reason`, `window`, `update`, raw `request`; `share` / `unshare` went with server-sent mod files on 2026-10-05); server — `store`, `refused`, and the test-only `inject_refused` and `clear_refused`. The client mod policy adds client `require`, `unrequire`, `require_reason`, `require_rule`, `policy_mode`, `unverified`, `approve`, `unapprove`, `approve_everything`, `approve_everything_confirm`, `approved_page`, `report`, and (CLIENTPOLICY3) `mid_session` and `alert_approve`; server `policy`, `notices`, the test-only `inject_report`, and (CLIENTPOLICY3) `recheck`. Reflection into Core's `JoinAdminTestBridge`; the server decides every request. Source-checked, built and game-free tested, not live-verified. | [bridge-ops-join.md](bridge-ops-join.md#join_admin) |
| `messages_record` | Both sides, main thread. Mod compatibility C5: records a mod's `ctx.Messages` traffic (sent and received on this side) as replay fixtures for `Progenitor.Testing` under `BepInEx/progenitor-dev/messages/<mod>/`: `on {mod, seconds, maxMessages, maxBytes}` (`mod` required, `*` for every mod; bounded: at most 1800 s, 5000 fixtures, 16 MiB, and 64 MiB for the folder across sessions; written by a background thread from a bounded queue that drops and counts when full), `off`, `status`. Reflection into Core's `ModMessageRecordBridge`; a Core swap ends a session. Source-checked and game-free tested, not run in the game. | [bridge-ops-join.md](bridge-ops-join.md#messages_record) |
| `join_ui` | Client, main thread. JOINFLOW1: read and drive the [join screens](join-flow.md) (server message, mods page, Thunderstore confirmation) through the buttons' own handlers: `read`, `press {button}`, `row {id}`, `recommend {key}`, `dont_show {on}`, `escape`, `show`, `preview`, `forget_seen`, `forget_trust` (the Thunderstore confirmations; `trust_tick` went with the trust prompt on 2026-10-05), `render {message, next, repeat, every, precision, passed, now}`; JOINFLOW2: `install`, `ts_install`, `join_now`, `restart {dryRun}` (the dry run writes `progenitor-rejoin.json` and reports the relaunch command without quitting), `later`, `cancel`, `rejoin_read`, `rejoin_clear`, `rejoin_now` (the after-relaunch pickup without a restart); JOINFLOW4: `versions` (the version store: kept copies hash-checked, the journal, the last join and restore), `versions_park {id, restart}`, `versions_swap {id, version, restart, forServer}`, `versions_restore {id, version}`, `versions_restore_all`, `versions_forget {id, version, force}`, `versions_prefer {id, version}`, `versions_reconcile`, `versions_evict`, `versions_plan`, and each required row's `kept`. Reflection into Core's `JoinFlowTestBridge`. JOINFLOW1 partly live-verified; JOINFLOW2 and JOINFLOW4 source-checked and game-free tested, not live-verified. | [bridge-ops-join.md](bridge-ops-join.md#join_ui) |
| `remote_install` | Either side, main thread. REMOTEINST1 admin remote installs: client `status` (route, server status, SteamID, a package's latest step) / `request` / `install` / `update` / `uninstall` / `cancel`; server `status` (bound `[RemoteInstall]` settings, busy, Thunderstore probe, ledger rows, audit tail) / `probe`. Reflection into Core like `install_local`; the server re-reads admin per request. Source-checked and game-free tested, not live-verified. [bridge-ops-client.md](bridge-ops-client.md#remote_install). |

The bridge does not bind at all unless `DevGate` passes: an explicit opt-in marker
(`BepInEx/config/progenitor-dev.enable`) or `PROGENITOR_DEV=1`, a loopback bind, no master-server
listing or public bind, and a positive private-instance signal. It refuses outright under the
maintainer's live-server root. See [`bridge.md`](bridge.md#the-opt-in-marker--the-bridge-refuses-to-start-without-it).

## Known dual surfaces

`ctx.Effects.Borrow` and `ctx.Borrow` (a `PluginContext` extension in `PluginContextEffects`) are the
same call — prefer `ctx.Effects.Borrow`. `ctx.Content.InvalidateEdits` is an extension method
(`PluginContentEdits`), not a member of `PluginContent`. `ctx.Castles.ForUser` takes an `Entity`
while the static `Castles.ForUser` takes a `CastleId`.

Data-only item packs load from `BepInEx/progenitor-content/<pack-id>/` on the server. They contain only validated JSON; no separate item plugin is required. The first example is the [bone sword pack](content-packs.md). This path is under development; the bone sword stat deltas, switching back, reconnect and ten-definition synchronization have limited live evidence in that guide. It does not deliver custom graphics.

Custom item ownership is retained for framework-managed quarantine after permanent mod removal. Admins can explicitly attempt best-effort restoration of supported stacks; unique equipment and unqualified destinations are preserved. See [item removal](item-removal.md) for triggers, preservation rules and current coverage.

## Appendix: framework-internal public types

The four assemblies declare 1001 public types. This appendix accounts for types outside the contract tables.
They are `public` for a stated reason and **carry no compatibility promise**: a name here can move namespace, change shape or become `internal` in any
commit, with no deprecation window and no row anywhere else in this file. Nothing in this appendix
is a thing to build a plugin against — if a mod needs one of these, that is a request for an API
row, not a license to reference it.

Counted 2026-09-05 (job J17) from `src/Progenitor.Core`, `src/Progenitor.Bootstrap` and
`src/Progenitor.Dev`, excluding `obj/` and `bin/`. A type counts as documented when this file names
it, so the appendix and the tables above together are the whole public surface — which is what
`ApiSurfaceTests` checks, so this list cannot quietly rot.

### What "why public" means

| Value | Means |
|---|---|
| `tests` | `tests/Progenitor.Tests` names the type. Core is deliberately split so that every decision with a rule in it (`*Policy`, `*Logic`, `*Rules`, `*Text`, the verdict enums) sits in a game-free class the plain xUnit suite can compile and drive without BepInEx, Unity or ProjectM. Visibility is what buys that. |
| `tests (via X)` | Not named in the suite itself, but nested in — or returned by — a type that is. |
| `Bootstrap-Core boundary` | Bootstrap is non-collectible and lives for the process; Core is collectible and swapped underneath it. Neither assembly may hold a compile-time reference to the other's types across a swap, so the two meet through reflection (`ProcessHost`), typed slots (`HarmonySlots`, `PatchSlots`, `UiSlots`, `ReceiveDetours`) and process-lifetime stores (`ContentMemory`, `IconCache`, `NativePinRegistry`). Both sides of each shape are public because the reflection lookup needs them to be. |
| `Dev bridge` | `Progenitor.Dev` is a separate assembly that is never shipped — the public zip omits it and `tools/package.ps1` asserts that. Its op classes and their parsed-argument types are public across that assembly boundary and nowhere else. |
| `generated` | Emitted by a generator (`tools/layoutgen`), not hand-written. |
| `Core-internal` | Public today, and neither the suite nor another assembly names it. These are the candidates for an accessibility change. |

**Making these `internal` is a separate decision and not a keyword edit.** 228 of the 323 rows
below are reached by the test project (219 named in it directly, 9 through an enclosing type), so
narrowing them means `InternalsVisibleTo` plus a sweep of the suite — a change to how Progenitor is
tested rather than to how it is documented. The 31 rows marked `Core-internal` are the ones nothing
outside their own assembly names, and so the only ones a keyword edit would reach today. This
appendix fences the surface; the accessibility question is left open for a later decision.

### Documented under another name

These sixteen types **are** contract. They appear in the tables above under the form a plugin
actually writes — an attribute usage, a `ctx` property, or a member on an interface — so they have
no row of their own and are not part of the appendix proper:

| Type | Written as |
|---|---|
| `CommandAttribute` / `CommandGroupAttribute` / `RemainderAttribute` | `[Command]` / `[CommandGroup]` / `[Remainder]` |
| `NetworkCompatibilityAttribute` / `ProgenitorSideAttribute` | `[NetworkCompatibility]` / `[ProgenitorSide]` |
| `PluginHooks` / `PluginHandshake` / `PluginPatches` / `PluginGodMode` / `PluginKeybinds` / `PluginWorldState` / `VirtualSystems` | `ctx.Hooks` / `ctx.Handshake` / `ctx.Patches` / `ctx.GodMode` / `ctx.Keybinds` / `ctx.WorldState` / `ctx.Systems` |
| `IUnitBehaviour` | `IUnitHandle.Behaviour` |
| `EntityExtensions` | the `Entity` `Exists`/`Has`/`Read`/… row |
| `CustomEventHandler` / `CustomEventFromClientHandler` | the delegate parameter of `OnReceive` / `OnReceiveFromClient` |

### One borderline class of row

Some rows below are the enum or struct half of a **documented** member's signature —
`UnitStepOutcome` under `UnitStepCompleted`, `CastleBlockReason` under `CastlePlacementCheck`,
`RebindOutcome` under `RebindResult`, `EffectHandleState` under `IEffectHandle`. They are here
because this file has no row of their own, not because a plugin may not name them. Promoting them
to contract rows is an open docs item, not a decision this appendix makes.

### The types

352 declarations — 323 distinct names, a few of them mirrored into Bootstrap — in 332 rows across
28 namespaces, one table per namespace, alphabetical inside each. Bootstrap's 21 `HarmonySlots`
delegates share a row because they are one mechanism. The compatibility-rewriter types appear twice
on purpose, once under `Progenitor.Compat` and once under `Progenitor.Bootstrap`: Bootstrap holds
its own copy because a type that must survive a Core swap cannot live in the collectible assembly.
Purpose lines are the first sentence of each type's own doc comment.

#### `Progenitor`

| Type | Purpose | Why public |
|---|---|---|
| `DiagnosticLog` | `LogDiag(this ManualLogSource, object)`: an Info line to `LogOutput.log` and the bridge ring, not the BepInEx console ([bridge.md](bridge.md#logging-policy)). Plugins may use it for their own per-frame diagnostics. | Dev bridge (gate and host_capture lines) |
| `BootstrapLink.UiDispatch` | Matches Bootstrap UiSlots.Dispatch. | Bootstrap-Core boundary |
| `BootstrapLink.UiKeyDispatch` | Matches Bootstrap UiInputContext.KeyDispatch. | Bootstrap-Core boundary |
| `BootstrapLink.UiMaskApply` | Matches Bootstrap UiMaskDetour.MaskApply. | Bootstrap-Core boundary |
| `Layout` | Numeric memory-layout facts read out of the game's metadata: field offsets, native array strides and enum values. | generated |
| `Runtime` | Static entry Bootstrap invokes after LoadFromStream of this assembly into a collectible ALC. | Bootstrap-Core boundary |

| `EntityLifecycleOps` | Debug-bridge `entity` op: `exists`, DIAG5 `destroy` (phase `buffs`: `DestroyUtility.Destroy(TryRemoveBuff)` + `DestroyTag` each still-existing `BuffBuffer` row; no Attach strip; no unit destroy), and `finalize` (`waiting-buffs` if a listed id still exists; `buffs` if a new live row is destroyed now, with `cycle`; `tagged` once listed ids are gone; `stuck` at 4 cycles without tagging). Every phase entry carries `staleRows` (buffer rows whose entity is gone). The unit is never `DestroyUtility.Destroy`'d before `tagged`, and there (and in `force`) it gets `DestroyUtility.Destroy` alone (`destroy: "replicated"`) so clients see the removal; `DestroyTag` is only a logged fallback after 120 frames ([bridge-ops.md](bridge-ops.md#entity)). Player/user/prefab refused. | Dev bridge |

## Experimental public additions (uncommitted)

These working-tree types have not landed; Bootstrap and Core are deployed only to an isolated test fixture. They are not a claim of working or production-safe native control. See [player forms](player-forms.md).

### `Progenitor.Players.Forms`

| Public type | Purpose and current availability |
|---|---|
| `PlayerForms` | `AvailableForms`, `IsReady`, and `TryBegin(World, Entity user, int prefabGuid, TimeSpan, string owner, out PlayerFormHandle?, out string)`. Guard readiness is separate from profile qualification; every normal request currently fails because no profile is qualified. |
| `PlayerFormDescriptor` | Read-only `Name`, `PrefabName`, `PrefabGuid`, `Verification`; only experimental Lord Styx is listed. |
| `PlayerFormVerification` | Evidence marker; currently `Experimental` only. |
| `PlayerFormHandle` | Read-only `Id` and diagnostic `State`, inspectable `TryReturn(out string)`, and `Dispose`. Accepted requests may be pending. Failed begin can also return a recovery handle. Failed return/dispose retains a retryable identifier; State preserves Bootstrap cleanup/world-ended/unknown status. |

Example: `samples/Progenitor.Sample/SampleForms.cs` (`.form`, `.form begin [name] [seconds]`, `.form return`). It lists `AvailableForms`, reads `IsReady`, calls `TryBegin`, and `ctx.Track`s every non-null handle. Source-checked and game-free tested (`SampleAuthorFacadeTests`); not live-verified. A begin on current main is expected to be refused because no profile is qualified.

Track every non-null handle with `ctx.Track(handle)`, even if `TryBegin` returns false; a partial begin can retain recovery work. The updated Bootstrap guards and Core facade are both required. Bootstrap update requires a server restart; there is no client update requirement for this addition. 29/29 policy and 5/5 facade tests pass; isolated startup guards, refusal and an ordinary save are verified, but no client/form activation or active recovery has been tested live. Do not bypass qualification.

#### `Progenitor.Combat`

| Type | Purpose | Why public |
|---|---|---|
| `CombatHooks` | Core install for `UnitDied` / `PlayerDied` / `DamageDealt` publishers (Kill postfix + StatChangeSystem After). | Core-internal |

#### `Progenitor.Servants`

| Type | Purpose | Why public |
|---|---|---|
| `ServantBridge` | The Dev bridge's `servants` op over the `ctx.Servants` code: list, zones, send, finish a hunt now, the servant-timers setting. Not a plugin API; plugins use `ctx.Servants`. | Dev bridge |
| `ServantDurationSlot` | One hunt length choice of the game's mission settings, as `servants zones` reports it. | Dev bridge |
| `ServantEmptyResult` | What `servants empty` did: per servant moved and left units, the containers tried. | Dev bridge |
| `ServantEmptyRow` | One servant of `servants empty`: before, moved, left, discarded and why not everything moved. | Dev bridge |
| `ServantFinishResult` | What `servants finish` did: method, reason, the hunts before and after, a pending timers restore. | Dev bridge |
| `ServantTimersStatus` | The game's servant-timers debug setting as `servants timers` reads or sets it. | Dev bridge |
| `ServantZoneInfo` | One map zone with a servant mission for a castle: discovered, active hunt, servant slots. | tests |
| `ServantZonesReport` | What `servants zones` found for a castle. | Dev bridge |



## Players directory

`ctx.Players` is the server player directory (`PlayerDirectory`): online enumeration and lookup by account id or display name. Ambiguous names return candidates. Session entity refs stay valid only while `PluginPlayers.IsFresh` is true. Read-only; unload has nothing to reverse.

Example: `SamplePlayerDirectory.cs` (`.players`, `.players name <displayName>`, `.players account <platformId>`) calls `Online`, `FindByName`, `TryGetByAccount`, `IsFresh` and `Revision`. Source-checked and game-free tested (`SampleAuthorFacadeTests`); not live-verified. An ambiguous name is listed and not picked.

| Member | Status | Intent | Example |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ctx.Players.OnlinePositions()` → `IReadOnlyList<PlayerPosition>` | landed, not live-verified | Where every online character stands now: the directory is re-read and one `Translation` is read per connected player with a live character, on every call; nothing runs between calls. For on-demand use or a slow tick, not per-frame polling. Players still loading in (no character) are left out. Server only; throws on the client. | `SamplePlayerDirectory.cs` (`.players where`) |
| `ctx.Players.TryGetPosition(PlayerInfo, out PlayerPosition)` | landed, not live-verified | One player's position now. The account is looked up again, so a kept row never answers for a player who has since disconnected: false when the account is not online now, has no character, the row is from an earlier world load, or the position cannot be read. Server only. | none (same read as `OnlinePositions`) |
| `PlayerPosition` | landed | `Player` (`PlayerInfo`), `X`, `Y`, `Z`, `Position` (`CastleVec3`, for `ICastleHandle.IsInside`), `HorizontalDistanceTo(x, z)`. A snapshot. | unit test `SortBrazierPositionTests` |
| `PlayerPositionMath` | landed | Game-free: `Horizontal`, `AnyWithin` (any player within a radius, inclusive), `Within` (those within, nearest first). | unit test `SortBrazierPositionTests` |
| `ctx.Players.State(PlayerInfo)` / `State(Entity user)` → `PlayerStateInfo` | experimental (game-free tested, not live-verified) | Read-only, server, main thread. `Status` (`PlayerStateStatus`: `Ok`, `NoCharacter`, `NotAPlayer`, `Stale`, `Unavailable`) and `Reason`; `Online`, `CharacterDisabled` (offline characters are disabled and keep their last buffs, so `InPvpCombat` can read true while offline), `Dead` (`Health.IsDead` or the `Dead` component), `Downed` (`Buff_General_Vampire_Wounded_Buff`, not dead), `InPvpCombat` (`Buff_InCombat_PvPVampire`), `InCombat` (PvP or `Buff_InCombat`), `Shapeshifted`, `ShapeshiftKind`, `ShapeshiftBuff`/`ShapeshiftBuffName`, `Health`/`MaxHealth`, `Standing` (ok, not dead, not downed). A stale `PlayerInfo`, a gone entity or a user entity now owned by another account is `Stale`; never throws for a bad player. Facts and limits: [modules.md, Player state](modules.md#player-state). | `SamplePlayerDirectory.cs` (`.players state [name]`); unit tests `PlayerStateLogicTests` |
| `PlayerShapeshiftKind` `None` / `Bat` / `Wolf` / `Bear` / `Rat` / `Toad` / `Human` / `Other` | experimental (game-free tested, not live-verified) | From the form buff's name (`AB_Shapeshift_<Form>_…`); vampire powers from the form menu (Blood Mend and the like) are not forms. `Other`: a form this enum does not name, or the controller's `Shapeshift.IsShapeshifted` with no named form buff. | unit tests `PlayerStateLogicTests` |


### Instance mutation kit

`UnitSpawnOptions` supports `LifetimeSeconds`, `TeamValue`, `FactionIndex`, `StripVBlood`, `Stats`, and a `Configure` callback. Handles expose `TrySetLifetime`, `TrySetTeam`, `TryStripVBlood`, and `TrySetStats`. These write the spawned entity only — not the shared prefab (`Content.Edit` / `PrefabRegistry.Edit`). A lifetime is stamped on the entity and enforced across plugin unload, Core swaps, world loads and restarts ([lifetime across reload and restart](modules.md#lifetime-across-reload-and-restart)).


### Camera preview

`PluginCamera.Observe(user, target, seconds)` leases a server streaming origin without moving the real character. Pair it with client `PluginCamera.Preview(point, yaw, seconds, ended, compressedHeight)`, which returns a `CameraPreview` orbit lease with manual rotation and automatic timeout. Dispose restores the player camera and render origin; unload releases the observer. Callers authorize targets, pair lease cleanup across the network, and capture gameplay input during preview. Remote ground-floor streaming and timeout restoration were verified live; upstairs visibility and interruption cases remain under qualification.

A preview can stay open until the player ends it: `CameraPreview.Hold()` (or `CameraPreviewOptions.Hold`) on the client and `CameraObservation.Hold()` / `Renew(seconds)` (or `CameraObserveOptions.Hold`) on the server. Both sides cap a hold at ten minutes, and both end on the safety rules in the rows above. Contract and the live check: [Camera preview](modules.md#camera-preview). The hold and the safety ends are source-checked and game-free tested, not live-verified.

`PluginCastles.TryGetBuildingPosition` reads the actual world position of a building, including elevation. Server-only; landed.

`IVanillaPanelTabs` is the owned container/station tab strip; on `StationPanel` it draws on workstations, refinement stations, prison cells and the devourer, and treats the unit stations (tomb, vermin nest, Nether Gate, by prefab name) as closed because they have no tab row (prison cell and devourer: source-checked; live check pending; [Station windows](ui.md#station-windows)). `StationQueuedCraft` describes queued recipe work. Both are landed contracts.

`PluginHud.Locate(position, label, seconds, arrivalDistance, showOnMaps)` adds optional world-map/minimap diamonds and removes the lease when the real character is within the specified 3D distance. The original overloads retain their behavior. `position` is world meters as `Translation` holds it (`y` up); the label's distance is the real 3D distance from the local character to `position`, never `arrivalDistance` ([parameters](ui.md#locate)). Two fixes (source-checked and game-free tested, `UiLocateTests`; not re-checked live): the character entity is re-found in each new world (a cache kept across a reconnect measured from whatever entity reused its id, so the label could read 3 m while the player stood 20 m away), and the marker is placed from the camera's viewport point scaled to the window (camera pixels were taken as window pixels). Map visibility, single-pin replacement and timed expiry and arrival removal were observed live. `PluginCamera.Preview` temporarily hides the native HUD and restores prior visibility on return (verified live). Castle station enumeration includes `ResearchStation`, including research desks without ordinary crafting recipes.
